Activity A: Complete the conversation with the past tense of the verb in brackets. Be careful with positive, negative, and interrogative.

A NEW LIFE

INSPECTOR GRANGER: OK Mr Thomas. Relax and tell me the problem.

MR THOMAS: It’s my wife Elizabeth. She went (go) out yesterday to buy some milk. And she \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (not/come) back.

INSPECTOR GRANGER: When \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (/you see) your wife for the last time?

MR THOMAS: Yesterday evening.

INSPECTOR GRANGER: Tell me what happened yesterday.

MR THOMAS: Well, we \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (wake up) at about 8.00 as usual. We \_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (have) breakfast and then I \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (drive) to work. I think she \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (go) shopping. It \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (be) a Wednesday. She always goes shopping on Wednesdays. Then she \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (make) dinner.

INSPECTOR GRANGER:  When \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ home? (/you come)

MR THOMAS: I \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (come) home at six o’clock. I \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (sit) down in my chair and \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (read) the newspaper. Then we \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (have) dinner. After dinner, my wife \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (go) to the kitchen. I \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (have) a glass of whisky and \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (sleep) in my chair.

INSPECTOR GRANGER: \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (/you speak) to your wife when you \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_(come) home? Or \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (/she say) anything to you?

MR THOMAS: I \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (not/speak) to her, but after about an hour she \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_(say), “This isn’t a life. I’m going out”. She \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (take) her bag and her coat. She \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (not/say) goodbye. I \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (hear) the door close. But I \_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ (not/see) her again.

INSPECTOR GRANGER : I see sir. I think I can guess the answer to this mystery.

Activity B: Read again, then answer the questions:

1\. What did Mr. Thomas’ wife go to buy?

2\. What time did Mr. Thomas’ and his wife wake up?

3\. What did Elizabeth do after shopping?

4\. What time did Mr. Thomas’ come home?

5\. Who drank a glass of whisky?

6\. What did Elizabeth take with her before leaving home?

\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_

Sarah went shopping alone.

Pauline sang a beautiful song.

Mark swam in the sea.

They ran in the marathon.

Caroline and Nick read a book.

Bob drove his car to work.

Daniel wrote a letter to his friend.

Mary cooked a delicious dinner.

Frak slept all the afternoon.

The children played happily in the park.

Greg walked the dog in the forest.

Tom built the house last summer.
